Saṁyutta Nikāya
4. Saḷāyatana Vagga
40. Moggallāna Saṁyutta

Sutta 11

Candano, Suyāma, Santusita, Sunimmita, Vasavatti

Adapted from the 1995 edition of the digital version of the Sri Lanka Buddha Jayanti Tripitaka Series.

 

I

Candano

 

[1][pts] Evaṁ me sutaṁ:|| ||

Ekaṁ samayaṁ āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno Sāvatthiyaṁ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme.|| ||

Atha kho āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno seyyathā pi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṁ vā bāhaṁ pasāreyya pasāritaṁ vā bāhaṁ sammiñjeyya,||
evam eva Jetavane antara-hito devesu Tāvatiṁsesu pātur ahosi.|| ||

Atha kho Candano devputto pañcahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||
upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Candano devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddha saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

■

"Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanahete kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

 

§

 

Atha kho Candano devputto chahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||
upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Candano devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddha saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

■

"Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanahete kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

 

§

 

Atha kho Candano devputto sattahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||
upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Candano devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddha saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

■

"Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanahete kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

 

§

 

Atha kho Candano devputto aṭṭhahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||
upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Candano devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddha saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

■

"Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanahete kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

 

§

 

Atha kho Candano devputto asītiyā devatāsahassehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||
upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Candano devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddha saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

■

"Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanahete kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

 

2.

 

Atha kho Candano devputto pañcahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami.|| ||

Upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Candano devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Iti pi so Bhagavā||
Arahaṁ||
Sammā Sambuddho||
vijjā-caraṇa-sampanno||
Sugato||
loka-vidū||
anuttaro purisa-damma-sārathī||
sattā deva-manussānaṁ||
Buddho||
Bhagavā' ti.|| ||

Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Svākhyāto Bhagavatā Dhammo sandiṭṭhiko||
akāliko||
ehi passiko||
opanayiko||
paccattaṁ||
veditabbo viññūhī' ti|| ||

Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Su-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
uju-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
ñāya-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
sāmīci-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho,||
yad idaṁ cattāri purisa-yugāni||
aṭṭhapurisa-puggalā.|| ||

Esa Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho.|| ||

Āhuneyo||
pāhuneyyo||
dakkhiṇeyyo||
añjali-karaṇīyo||
anuttaraṁ puñña-k-khettaṁ lokassā' ti.|| ||

Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Akhaṇḍehi||
acchiddehi||
asabalehi||
akammāsehi||
bhujissehi||
viññūppasatthehi;||
aparām-aṭṭhehi samādhi-saṁvaṭṭanikehi' ti.|| ||

Ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

■

"Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Iti pi so Bhagavā||
Arahaṁ||
Sammā Sambuddho||
vijjā-caraṇa-sampanno||
Sugato||
loka-vidū||
anuttaro purisa-damma-sārathī||
sattā deva-manussānaṁ||
Buddho||
Bhagavā' ti.|| ||

Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti,|| ||

'Svākhyāto Bhagavatā Dhammo sandiṭṭhiko||
akāliko||
ehi passiko||
opanayiko||
paccattaṁ||
veditabbo viññūhī' ti|| ||

Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Su-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
uju-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
ñāya-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
sāmīci-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho,||
yad idaṁ cattāri purisa-yugāni||
aṭṭhapurisa-puggalā.|| ||

Esa Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho.|| ||

Āhuneyo||
pāhuneyyo||
dakkhiṇeyyo||
añjali-karaṇīyo||
anuttaraṁ puñña-k-khettaṁ lokassā' ti.|| ||

Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Akhaṇḍehi||
acchiddehi||
asabalehi||
akammāsehi||
bhujissehi||
viññūppasatthehi;||
aparām-aṭṭhehi samādhi-saṁvaṭṭanikehi' ti.|| ||

Ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

 

§

 

Atha kho Candano devputto chahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami.|| ||

aṭṭhapurisa-puggalā.|| ||

Āhuneyo||
pāhuneyyo||
dakkhiṇeyyo||
añjali-karaṇīyo||
anuttaraṁ puñña-k-khettaṁ lokassā' ti.|| ||

Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Akhaṇḍehi||
acchiddehi||
asabalehi||
akammāsehi||
bhujissehi||
viññūppasatthehi;||
aparām-aṭṭhehi samādhi-saṁvaṭṭanikehi' ti.|| ||

Ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

■

"Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Iti pi so Bhagavā||
Arahaṁ||
Sammā Sambuddho||
vijjā-caraṇa-sampanno||
Sugato||
loka-vidū||
anuttaro purisa-damma-sārathī||
sattā deva-manussānaṁ||

Bhagavā' ti.|| ||

Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti,|| ||

'Svākhyāto Bhagavatā Dhammo sandiṭṭhiko||
akāliko||
ehi passiko||
opanayiko||
paccattaṁ||
veditabbo viññūhī' ti|| ||

Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Su-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
uju-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
ñāya-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
sāmīci-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho,||
yad idaṁ cattāri purisa-yugāni||
aṭṭhapurisa-puggalā.|| ||

Esa Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho.|| ||

Āhuneyo||
pāhuneyyo||
dakkhiṇeyyo||
añjali-karaṇīyo||
anuttaraṁ puñña-k-khettaṁ lokassā' ti.|| ||

Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Akhaṇḍehi||
acchiddehi||
asabalehi||
akammāsehi||
bhujissehi||
viññūppasatthehi;||
aparām-aṭṭhehi samādhi-saṁvaṭṭanikehi' ti.|| ||

Ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

3.

 

Atha kho āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno seyyathā pi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṁ vā bāhaṁ pasāreyya pasāritaṁ vā bāhaṁ sammiñjeyya,||
evam eva Jetavane antara-hito devesu Tāvatiṁsesu pātur ahosi.|| ||

Atha kho Candano devputto pañcahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||
upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Candano devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti.|| ||

Buddha saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti.

Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti.|| ||

Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi" ti.|| ||

 

4.

 

Atha kho Candano devputto pañcahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami.|| ||

Upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Candano devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Iti pi so Bhagavā||
arahaṁ||
Arahaṁ||
Sammā Sambuddho||
vijjā-caraṇa-sampanno||
Sugato||
loka-vidū||
anuttaro purisa-damma-sārathī||
sattā deva-manussānaṁ||
Buddho||
Bhagavā' ti.|| ||

Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantti|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Svākhyāto Bhagavatā Dhammo||
sandiṭṭhiko||
akāliko||
ehi passiko||
opanayiko||
paccattaṁ||
veditabbo viññūhī' ti|| ||

dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

'Su-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
uju-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
ñāya-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
sāmīci-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho,||
yad idaṁ cattāri purisa-yugāni||
aṭṭhapurisa-puggalā.|| ||

Esa Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho.|| ||

Āhuneyo||
pāhuneyyo||
dakkhiṇeyyo||
añjali-karaṇīyo||
anuttaraṁ puñña-k-khettaṁ lokassā' ti.|| ||

Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Akhaṇḍehi||
acchiddehi||
asabalehi||
akammāsehi||
bhujissehi||
viññūppasatthehi;||
aparām-aṭṭhehi samādhi-saṁvaṭṭanikehi' ti.|| ||

Ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi" ti.|| ||

 

II

Suyāma

 

Atha kho Suyāma devputto pañcahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||
upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Suyāma devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddha saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

■

"Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanahete kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

 

§

 

Atha kho Suyāma devputto chahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||
upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||

evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

evam idh'ekacce sattā||

sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||

evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

2.

 

Atha kho Suyāma devputto pañcahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami.|| ||

Upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Suyāma devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Iti pi so Bhagavā||
Arahaṁ||
Sammā Sambuddho||
vijjā-caraṇa-sampanno||
Sugato||
loka-vidū||
anuttaro purisa-damma-sārathī||
sattā deva-manussānaṁ||
Buddho||
Bhagavā' ti.|| ||

Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Svākhyāto Bhagavatā Dhammo sandiṭṭhiko||
akāliko||
ehi passiko||
opanayiko||
paccattaṁ||
veditabbo viññūhī' ti|| ||

Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

yad idaṁ cattāri purisa-yugāni||
aṭṭhapurisa-puggalā.|| ||

pāhuneyyo||
dakkhiṇeyyo||
añjali-karaṇīyo||
anuttaraṁ puñña-k-khettaṁ lokassā' ti.|| ||

Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

3.

 

Atha kho āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno seyyathā pi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṁ vā bāhaṁ pasāreyya pasāritaṁ vā bāhaṁ sammiñjeyya,||
evam eva Jetavane antara-hito devesu Tāvatiṁsesu pātur ahosi.|| ||

Atha kho Suyāma devputto pañcahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||
upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Suyāma devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti.|| ||

Buddha saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti.|| ||

Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanahete kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti.|| ||

Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjant.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi" ti.|| ||

 

4.

 

Atha kho Suyāma devputto pañcahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami.|| ||

Upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Suyāma devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Iti pi so Bhagavā||
arahaṁ||
Arahaṁ||
Sammā Sambuddho||
vijjā-caraṇa-sampanno||
Sugato||
loka-vidū||
anuttaro purisa-damma-sārathī||
sattā deva-manussānaṁ||
Buddho||
Bhagavā' ti.|| ||

Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantti|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Svākhyāto Bhagavatā Dhammo||
sandiṭṭhiko||
akāliko||
ehi passiko||
opanayiko||
paccattaṁ||
veditabbo viññūhī' ti|| ||

Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||

'Svākhyāto Bhagavatā Dhammo||
sandiṭṭhiko||
akāliko||
ehi passiko||
opanayiko||
paccattaṁ||
veditabbo viññūhī' ti|| ||

Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Su-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
uju-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
ñāya-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
sāmīci-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho,||
yad idaṁ cattāri purisa-yugāni||
aṭṭhapurisa-puggalā.|| ||

Esa Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho.|| ||

Āhuneyo||
pāhuneyyo||
dakkhiṇeyyo||
añjali-karaṇīyo||
anuttaraṁ puñña-k-khettaṁ lokassā' ti.|| ||

acchiddehi||

akammāsehi||
bhujissehi||
viññūppasatthehi;||

Ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||

sattā deva-manussānaṁ||
Buddho||
Bhagavā' ti.|| ||

Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantti|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti,|| ||

'Svākhyāto Bhagavatā Dhammo||
sandiṭṭhiko||
akāliko||
ehi passiko||
opanayiko||
paccattaṁ||
veditabbo viññūhī' ti|| ||

Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti,|| ||

'Su-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
uju-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
ñāya-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
sāmīci-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho,||
yad idaṁ cattāri purisa-yugāni||
aṭṭhapurisa-puggalā.|| ||

Esa Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho.|| ||

Āhuneyo||
pāhuneyyo||
dakkhiṇeyyo||
añjali-karaṇīyo||
anuttaraṁ puñña-k-khettaṁ lokassā' ti.|| ||

Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Akhaṇḍehi||
acchiddehi||
asabalehi||
akammāsehi||
bhujissehi||
viññūppasatthehi;||
aparām-aṭṭhehi samādhi-saṁvaṭṭanikehi' ti.|| ||

Ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi" ti.|| ||

 

III

Santusita

 

Atha kho Santusita devputto pañcahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||
upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Santusita devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddha saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

■

"Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanahete kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

 

§

 

Atha kho Santusita devputto chahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||
upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

evam idh'ekacce sattā||

evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||

sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

Upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Santusita devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Iti pi so Bhagavā||
Arahaṁ||
Sammā Sambuddho||
vijjā-caraṇa-sampanno||
Sugato||
loka-vidū||
anuttaro purisa-damma-sārathī||
sattā deva-manussānaṁ||
Buddho||
Bhagavā' ti.|| ||

Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Svākhyāto Bhagavatā Dhammo sandiṭṭhiko||
akāliko||
ehi passiko||
opanayiko||
paccattaṁ||
veditabbo viññūhī' ti|| ||

Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Su-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
uju-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
ñāya-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
sāmīci-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho,||
yad idaṁ cattāri purisa-yugāni||
aṭṭhapurisa-puggalā.|| ||

Esa Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho.|| ||

Āhuneyo||
pāhuneyyo||
dakkhiṇeyyo||
añjali-karaṇīyo||
anuttaraṁ puñña-k-khettaṁ lokassā' ti.|| ||

Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Akhaṇḍehi||
acchiddehi||
asabalehi||
akammāsehi||
bhujissehi||
viññūppasatthehi;||
aparām-aṭṭhehi samādhi-saṁvaṭṭanikehi' ti.|| ||

Ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||

sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

Esa Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho.|| ||

Āhuneyo||
pāhuneyyo||
dakkhiṇeyyo||
añjali-karaṇīyo||
anuttaraṁ puñña-k-khettaṁ lokassā' ti.|| ||

Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Akhaṇḍehi||
acchiddehi||
asabalehi||
akammāsehi||
bhujissehi||
viññūppasatthehi;||
aparām-aṭṭhehi samādhi-saṁvaṭṭanikehi' ti.|| ||

Ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

 

3.

 

Atha kho āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno seyyathā pi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṁ vā bāhaṁ pasāreyya pasāritaṁ vā bāhaṁ sammiñjeyya,||
evam eva Jetavane antara-hito devesu Tāvatiṁsesu pātur ahosi.|| ||

Atha kho Santusita devputto pañcahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||
upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Santusita devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti.|| ||

Buddha saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti.

Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

-◦-

dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi" ti.|| ||

Atha kho Santusita devputto pañcahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami.|| ||

dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi" ti.|| ||

Atha kho Santusita devputto sattahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Santusita devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

Bhagavā' ti.|| ||

evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantti|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||

dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Akhaṇḍehi||
acchiddehi||
asabalehi||
akammāsehi||
bhujissehi||
viññūppasatthehi;||
aparām-aṭṭhehi samādhi-saṁvaṭṭanikehi' ti.|| ||

Ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi" ti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti,|| ||

'Iti pi so Bhagavā||
arahaṁ||
Arahaṁ||
Sammā Sambuddho||
vijjā-caraṇa-sampanno||
Sugato||
loka-vidū||
anuttaro purisa-damma-sārathī||
sattā deva-manussānaṁ||
Buddho||
Bhagavā' ti.|| ||

Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantti|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti,|| ||

'Svākhyāto Bhagavatā Dhammo||
sandiṭṭhiko||
akāliko||
ehi passiko||
opanayiko||
paccattaṁ||
veditabbo viññūhī' ti|| ||

Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti,|| ||

'Su-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
uju-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
ñāya-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
sāmīci-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho,||
yad idaṁ cattāri purisa-yugāni||
aṭṭhapurisa-puggalā.|| ||

Esa Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho.|| ||

Āhuneyo||
pāhuneyyo||
dakkhiṇeyyo||
añjali-karaṇīyo||
anuttaraṁ puñña-k-khettaṁ lokassā' ti.|| ||

Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||

dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Akhaṇḍehi||
acchiddehi||
asabalehi||
akammāsehi||
bhujissehi||
viññūppasatthehi;||
aparām-aṭṭhehi samādhi-saṁvaṭṭanikehi' ti.|| ||

Ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi" ti.|| ||

 

IV

Sunimmita

 

Atha kho Sunimmita devputto pañcahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||
upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Sunimmita devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddha saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

■

"Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanahete kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

 

§

 

Atha kho Sunimmita devputto chahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||
upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Sunimmita devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddha saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

■

"Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanahete kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

 

§

 

Atha kho Sunimmita devputto sattahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Sunimmita devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||

evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

Atha kho Sunimmita devputto aṭṭhahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||

sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

Atha kho Sunimmita devputto asītiyā devatāsahassehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||

sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Su-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
uju-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
ñāya-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
sāmīci-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho,||
yad idaṁ cattāri purisa-yugāni||
aṭṭhapurisa-puggalā.|| ||

Esa Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho.|| ||

Āhuneyo||
pāhuneyyo||
dakkhiṇeyyo||
añjali-karaṇīyo||
anuttaraṁ puñña-k-khettaṁ lokassā' ti.|| ||

Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Akhaṇḍehi||
acchiddehi||
asabalehi||
akammāsehi||
bhujissehi||
viññūppasatthehi;||
aparām-aṭṭhehi samādhi-saṁvaṭṭanikehi' ti.|| ||

evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

■

"Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Iti pi so Bhagavā||
Arahaṁ||
Sammā Sambuddho||
vijjā-caraṇa-sampanno||
Sugato||
loka-vidū||
anuttaro purisa-damma-sārathī||
sattā deva-manussānaṁ||
Buddho||
Bhagavā' ti.|| ||

Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti,|| ||

'Svākhyāto Bhagavatā Dhammo sandiṭṭhiko||
akāliko||
ehi passiko||
opanayiko||
paccattaṁ||
veditabbo viññūhī' ti|| ||

Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Su-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
uju-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
ñāya-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
sāmīci-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho,||
yad idaṁ cattāri purisa-yugāni||
aṭṭhapurisa-puggalā.|| ||

Esa Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho.|| ||

Āhuneyo||
pāhuneyyo||
dakkhiṇeyyo||
añjali-karaṇīyo||
anuttaraṁ puñña-k-khettaṁ lokassā' ti.|| ||

Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Akhaṇḍehi||
acchiddehi||
asabalehi||
akammāsehi||
bhujissehi||
viññūppasatthehi;||
aparām-aṭṭhehi samādhi-saṁvaṭṭanikehi' ti.|| ||

Ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

 

3.

 

Atha kho āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno seyyathā pi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṁ vā bāhaṁ pasāreyya pasāritaṁ vā bāhaṁ sammiñjeyya,||
evam eva Jetavane antara-hito devesu Tāvatiṁsesu pātur ahosi.|| ||

Atha kho Sunimmita devputto pañcahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||
upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Sunimmita devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti.|| ||

Buddha saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjant.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi" ti.|| ||

 

§

 

Atha kho Sunimmita devputto asītiyā devatāsahassehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||

4.

 

Atha kho Sunimmita devputto pañcahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Sunimmita devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Iti pi so Bhagavā||
arahaṁ||
Arahaṁ||
Sammā Sambuddho||
vijjā-caraṇa-sampanno||
Sugato||
loka-vidū||
anuttaro purisa-damma-sārathī||
sattā deva-manussānaṁ||
Buddho||
Bhagavā' ti.|| ||

Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantti|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||

dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

sandiṭṭhiko||
akāliko||
ehi passiko||
opanayiko||
paccattaṁ||
veditabbo viññūhī' ti|| ||

yad idaṁ cattāri purisa-yugāni||
aṭṭhapurisa-puggalā.|| ||

Esa Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho.|| ||

Āhuneyo||
pāhuneyyo||
dakkhiṇeyyo||
añjali-karaṇīyo||
anuttaraṁ puñña-k-khettaṁ lokassā' ti.|| ||

Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Akhaṇḍehi||
acchiddehi||
asabalehi||
akammāsehi||
bhujissehi||
viññūppasatthehi;||
aparām-aṭṭhehi samādhi-saṁvaṭṭanikehi' ti.|| ||

dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi" ti.|| ||

dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

Sādhu kho devputto Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Svākhyāto Bhagavatā Dhammo||
sandiṭṭhiko||
akāliko||
ehi passiko||
opanayiko||
paccattaṁ||
veditabbo viññūhī' ti|| ||

Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Su-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
uju-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
ñāya-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
sāmīci-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho,||
yad idaṁ cattāri purisa-yugāni||
aṭṭhapurisa-puggalā.|| ||

Esa Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho.|| ||

Āhuneyo||
pāhuneyyo||
dakkhiṇeyyo||
añjali-karaṇīyo||
anuttaraṁ puñña-k-khettaṁ lokassā' ti.|| ||

Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||

dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||

dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

-◦-

'Akhaṇḍehi||
acchiddehi||
asabalehi||
akammāsehi||
bhujissehi||
viññūppasatthehi;||
aparām-aṭṭhehi samādhi-saṁvaṭṭanikehi' ti.|| ||

Ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||

V

Vasavatti

 

Atha kho Vasavatti devputto pañcahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||
upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Vasavatti devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddha saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

■

"Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Dhammaṁ saraṇa-gamanahete kho mārisa Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti,||
Saṅghaṁ saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho Moggallāna||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

 

§

2.

Sammā Sambuddho||
vijjā-caraṇa-sampanno||
Sugato||
loka-vidū||
anuttaro purisa-damma-sārathī||
sattā deva-manussānaṁ||
Buddho||
Bhagavā' ti.|| ||

evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Svākhyāto Bhagavatā Dhammo sandiṭṭhiko||
akāliko||
ehi passiko||
opanayiko||
paccattaṁ||
veditabbo viññūhī' ti|| ||

sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

'Su-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||

yad idaṁ cattāri purisa-yugāni||
aṭṭhapurisa-puggalā.|| ||

pāhuneyyo||
dakkhiṇeyyo||
añjali-karaṇīyo||
anuttaraṁ puñña-k-khettaṁ lokassā' ti.|| ||

Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

paccattaṁ||
veditabbo viññūhī' ti|| ||

Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Su-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
uju-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
ñāya-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
sāmīci-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho,||
yad idaṁ cattāri purisa-yugāni||
aṭṭhapurisa-puggalā.|| ||

Esa Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho.|| ||

Āhuneyo||
pāhuneyyo||
dakkhiṇeyyo||
añjali-karaṇīyo||
anuttaraṁ puñña-k-khettaṁ lokassā' ti.|| ||

Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Akhaṇḍehi||
acchiddehi||
asabalehi||
akammāsehi||
bhujissehi||
viññūppasatthehi;||
aparām-aṭṭhehi samādhi-saṁvaṭṭanikehi' ti.|| ||

Ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī" ti.|| ||

"Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Iti pi so Bhagavā||
Arahaṁ||
Sammā Sambuddho||
vijjā-caraṇa-sampanno||
Sugato||
loka-vidū||
anuttaro purisa-damma-sārathī||
sattā deva-manussānaṁ||
Buddho||
Bhagavā' ti.|| ||

evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī.|| ||

paccattaṁ||
veditabbo viññūhī' ti|| ||

3.

 

Atha kho āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno seyyathā pi nāma balavā puriso sammiñjitaṁ vā bāhaṁ pasāreyya pasāritaṁ vā bāhaṁ sammiñjeyya,||
evam eva Jetavane antara-hito devesu Tāvatiṁsesu pātur ahosi.|| ||

Atha kho Vasavatti devputto pañcahi devatāsatehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami,||
upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Vasavatti devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti.|| ||

Buddha saraṇa-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

evam idh'ekacce sattā||

sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi" ti.|| ||

"Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Buddhaṁ saraṇa-gamanaṁ hoti.|| ||

dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

§

 

Atha kho Vasavatti devputto asītiyā devatāsahassehi saddhiṁ yen'āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno ten'upasaṅkami.|| ||

Upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṁ Mahā Moggallānaṁ abhivādetvā eka-m-antaṁ aṭṭhāsi.|| ||

Eka-m-antaṁ ṭhitaṁ kho Vasavatti devputto āyasmā Mahā Moggallāno etad avoca:|| ||

"Sādhu kho devputto Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Iti pi so Bhagavā||
arahaṁ||
Arahaṁ||
Sammā Sambuddho||
vijjā-caraṇa-sampanno||
Sugato||
loka-vidū||
anuttaro purisa-damma-sārathī||
sattā deva-manussānaṁ||
Buddho||
Bhagavā' ti.|| ||

Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantti|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Svākhyāto Bhagavatā Dhammo||
sandiṭṭhiko||
akāliko||
ehi passiko||
opanayiko||
paccattaṁ||
veditabbo viññūhī' ti|| ||

Dhamme avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Su-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
uju-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
ñāya-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho||
sāmīci-paṭipanno Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho,||
yad idaṁ cattāri purisa-yugāni||
aṭṭhapurisa-puggalā.|| ||

Esa Bhagavato sāvaka-saṅgho.|| ||

Āhuneyo||
pāhuneyyo||
dakkhiṇeyyo||
añjali-karaṇīyo||
anuttaraṁ puñña-k-khettaṁ lokassā' ti.|| ||

Saṅghe avecca pasādena samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjanti.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho devputto ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamanaṁ hoti:|| ||

'Akhaṇḍehi||
acchiddehi||
asabalehi||
akammāsehi||
bhujissehi||
viññūppasatthehi;||
aparām-aṭṭhehi samādhi-saṁvaṭṭanikehi' ti.|| ||

Ariya-kantehi sīlehi samannā-gamana-hetu kho devputto||
evam idh'ekacce sattā||
kāyassa bhedā param māraṇā||
sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantī.|| ||

Te aññe deve dasahi ṭhānehi adhigaṇhanti:||
dibbena āyunā,||
dibbena vaṇṇena,||
dibbena sukhena,||
dibbena yasena,||
dibbena ādhipateyyena,||
dibbehi rūpehi,||
dibbehi saddehi,||
dibbehi gandhehi,||
dibbehi rasehi,||
dibbehi phoṭṭhabbehi" ti.|| ||

-◦-

Sādhu kho mārisa Moggallāna Buddhe avecca pasādena samannā-gamanaṁ hoti,|| ||

'Iti pi so Bhagavā||

Bhagavā' ti.|| ||

sugatiṁ saggaṁ lokaṁ upapajjantti|| ||
